### [1] [''Bless his heart... he thought all we did was push a button": Understanding Worker Challenges with U.S. Election Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19233)
*Delaney Gomen,Josiah Hester,Naveena Karusala,Michael Specter*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对20个州50名选举官员的访谈与问卷数据进行定性分析，本文发现选举技术的设计缺陷是导致操作失误的重要成因，并进一步将技术设计带来的伤害关联到对民主的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国选举官员所依赖的技术本身也在给他们的工作带来挑战。每次选举中，因使用选举技术而产生的失误会衍生出误导性叙事，进而威胁选举官员的人身安全并侵蚀公众对选举的信任。作者希望直接从工作人员的视角理解这些挑战的影响。

Method: 采用定性分析方法，结合访谈与问卷数据，样本为来自20个州的共50名选举官员。

Result: 研究发现，设计是导致使用选举技术时出现失误的一个实质性因素；但资金不足和技术支持缺乏等其他障碍也给选举工作带来压力。研究展示了选举技术设计如何对选举官员造成伤害，并将这些伤害与其对民主的负面影响联系起来。

Conclusion: 本文强调，人本计算领域的研究者可以通过推动选举技术的整体可用性改进，来支持美国选举官员，进而支持民主。

Abstract: This paper explores how the technologies U.S. election officials depend on also challenge their work. Every election, misleading narratives stem from errors that occur while using election technology, threatening election official safety and eroding faith in elections. We seek to understand the impact of these challenges directly from worker perspectives. This paper presents a qualitative analysis using combined interview and survey data from a total of 50 election officials representing 20 states. Our findings present design as a substantial factor in mistakes when using election technology, but other obstacles, like lack of funding and technical support, also strain operations. We show how election technology design can lead to harms for election officials and connect those harms to their negative effects on democracy. Our work emphasizes the potential for researchers in human-centered computing to support U.S. election officials-and democracy-by working towards better usability across election technologies.

</details>


### [2] [MuTable: Composable and Reusable Table Transformations for In-Situ Data Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19294)
*Fuling Sun,Devamardeep Hayatpur,Jane L. E,Nicole Sultanum,Haijun Xia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MuTable 是一个原型系统，将数据变换具体化为持久、可组合、可复用的修饰器，使用户能在表格内部就地探索数据，并随问题演变保留和调整中间形态，从而减少表格与可视化之间的协调成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格适合精确查找和细节查看，但在概览与模式发现任务上存在局限，因此常需创建可视化以获得更丰富的感知支持。实践中在表格与图表间切换往往需要维护平行表征，带来上下文切换和额外的协调工作。

Method: 在已有混合表格—可视化系统的基础上，提出 MuTable 原型。其核心是将数据变换具体化为持久（persistent）、可组合（composable）、可复用（reusable）的修饰器，支持就地（in-situ）数据探索；用户可在问题演变过程中重塑表格，同时保留并适配中间形态。评估方式为对八位数据工作者的专家访谈。

Result: 专家访谈表明，MuTable 能够支持不同表征之间的协调、快速探索，以及用户在构建可视化时更大的自主性，并可作为低承诺（low-commitment）的探索空间。

Conclusion: MuTable 通过持久、可组合、可复用的变换修饰器，将表格与可视化的协调工作内化到表格本身，为数据工作者提供了一个支持快速探索与表征协调的低门槛探索环境。

Abstract: Tables are central to data work to support precise lookup and full detail, but they can be limiting for overview and pattern-finding tasks. Visualizations are then created to gain richer perceptual support. In practice, moving between tables and charts often requires maintaining parallel representations, introducing context switching, and extra coordination work. Building on prior hybrid table-visualization systems, we present MuTable, a prototype that reifies transformations as persistent, composable, and reusable modifiers to support in-situ data exploration. Users can reshape the table while retaining and adapting intermediate forms as their questions evolve. An expert interview with eight data workers suggests that MuTable can support coordination between representations, rapid exploration, and greater user agency in constructing visualizations, as a low-commitment exploration space.

</details>


### [3] ["I Know Where to Look," But Does the LLM? Charting the Gaps Between Clinical Expert Needs and Unstructured Data Abstraction Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19318)
*Venkatesh Sivaraman,Rigney Turnham,George Bonano,Nevin Aresh,Renumathy Dhanasekaran,Margaret Guo,Sindhu Kubendran,Olivia Lin,Jonathan D Louie,Kristan Olazo,Jeanne Shen,Harish Vasudevan,Jeanette Wong,Emily Alsentzer,Jason A Fries,Anobel Odisho,John Gordan,Jean Feng,Julian C Hong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文与癌症研究团队共同设计了基于LLM的交互式临床数据抽取系统Libretto，并评估其在真实研究问题中的可用性。研究发现，临床医生虽能凭直觉标注复杂概念，但在14项任务中的12项难以用LLM复现这些直觉，需对信息抽取流程做出根本调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床数据抽取对癌症等疾病的知识推进至关重要，LLM信息抽取有望加速这一过程。然而，当前框架是否能有效支持缺乏AI专业知识的临床研究者尚不明确，这一缺口促使作者开展研究。

Method: 作者与七个癌症研究团队共同设计了一个基于LLM的交互式抽取系统Libretto，随后通过评估该系统帮助团队回答真实世界研究问题的能力来检验其实用性。

Result: 临床医生知道在患者记录中何处以及如何标注复杂概念，但在14项任务中有12项无法将这种直觉迁移到LLM上。主要障碍包括上下文相关的记录可靠性判断、难以操控"氛围编程"式提示，以及缺乏灵活性的评估策略，这些都要求对信息抽取工作流做出根本性改变。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了HCI领域需要解决的开放性问题，以弥合AI数据工作工具与临床用户需求之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Clinical data abstraction, the process of distilling structured information from patient records, plays a key role in advancing knowledge about diseases such as cancer. Information extraction (IE) with large language models (LLMs) could accelerate this process, but it is unclear whether current frameworks effectively support clinical researchers without AI expertise. To address this, we co-designed an interactive LLM-based abstraction system called Libretto with seven cancer research teams, then evaluated the system's ability to help them answer real-world research questions. We found that while clinicians knew where and how to annotate complex concepts in patient notes, in twelve of fourteen tasks they faced barriers to replicating those intuitions with LLMs. Contextual note reliability judgments, difficulties in steering vibe-coded prompts, and inflexible evaluation strategies necessitated fundamental changes to the IE workflow. Our results highlight open problems for HCI research to bridge the gaps between AI data work tools and clinical users' needs.

</details>


### [4] [Durably Reducing Belief in Women's Health Misinformation Through Culturally Adaptive AI Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19364)
*Anku Rani,Kokil Jaidka,Shruti Sharma,Pragya Mahajan,Manisha Wadhwa,Andrew B. Lippman,Pattie Maes,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究在印度郊区低识字女性中开展实地实验，发现文化适配的AI生成视频能显著降低健康错误信息信念，效果约为中性版本的近两倍，并持续三周。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康错误信息对女性伤害尤为严重，但现有干预很少触及维持错误信念的社区规范；研究希望检验文化适配的AI视频能否在低识字女性中减少健康错误信息信念。

Method: 在印度郊区开展实地实验（N=434），参与者观看由AI生成、呈现者为文化适配或中性形象的视频，并与无干预对照组比较；实验后进行访谈。

Result: 文化适配呈现者使错误信息信念降低30%，接近中性呈现者效果的两倍；效果持续三周，且在社区强化的信念（如将不孕归咎于女性）上优势最大，在医学知识缺口（如疫苗理解）上几乎无效。

Conclusion: 文化适配的AI干预有望对抗社会嵌入型健康错误信息，尤其适用于受社区规范支撑的错误信念。

Abstract: Health misinformation disproportionately harms women, yet interventions rarely address the community norms that sustain false beliefs. We test whether culturally adaptive AI-generated video in which the presenter looks like someone from her community reduces misinformation belief among low-literacy women in suburban India. In a field experiment (N=434), participants watched an AI-generated video featuring either an adaptive or neutral presenter. The culturally adaptive presenter reduced misinformation belief by 30%, nearly twice the reduction produced by the neutral presenter compared to the non-intervention control condition. Post-experiment interviews suggest women recalled the neutral condition as a generic video but recognized the adaptive presenter. Gains persisted for three weeks. The adaptive advantage was largest for beliefs reinforced by community, such as blaming women for infertility, and negligible for medical knowledge gaps, such as understanding vaccines. These findings demonstrate the potential of culturally adaptive AI interventions to counter socially embedded health misinformation.

</details>


### [5] [Use and Effects of LLMs in Peer Review: A Randomized Experiment and Survey at ICML 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19420)
*Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Wesley Hanwen Deng,Jennifer Wortman Vaughan,Buxin Su,Weijie Su,Alekh Agarwal,Sharon Li,Martin Jaggi,Daniel G. Goldstein,Nihar B. Shah,Miroslav Dudík*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过ICML 2026的大规模随机实验与匿名调研，发现LLM使用政策对论文决策、评分和审稿人信心几乎无影响，但审稿意见字数增加，且政策违规现象普遍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正快速重塑同行评审流程，因此有必要理解审稿人在实践中如何使用LLM，以及不同的LLM使用政策会如何影响评审结果。

Method: 在ICML 2026（涉及超24,000篇论文、17,000名审稿人）开展随机实验与匿名事后调研。审稿人被随机分配到两种政策之一：禁止一切LLM使用的保守政策，或允许有限辅助的宽松政策；随机化在部分主赛道论文和审稿人中进行。事后调研回收有效样本N=1,486。

Result: 政策分配对最终论文决策、论文评分和审稿人信心几乎无影响，但宽松政策下的审稿意见长度增加5.5%–7%。调研显示审稿人对LLM态度多元，且存在大量违规：保守政策审稿人中22.5%承认使用LLM，宽松政策审稿人中36.5%报告至少一次被明确禁止的用法。

Conclusion: 作者讨论了这些发现对未来同行评审政策制定与工具设计的启示，指出政策约束与实际行为之间存在明显落差，需重新思考评审规范与工具支持方式。

Abstract: LLMs are rapidly reshaping peer review, making it important to understand how reviewers use them in practice and how different LLM-use policies affect review outcomes. We investigate these questions through a randomized experiment and an anonymous post-survey at ICML 2026, a major machine learning conference involving over 24,000 papers and 17,000 reviewers. Reviewers were assigned to either a conservative policy prohibiting all LLM use or a permissive policy allowing limited assistance, with randomization among a subset of main-track papers and reviewers. Policy assignment had near-zero effects on final paper decisions, paper scores, and reviewer confidence, although reviews under the permissive policy were 5.5-7% longer. Post-survey responses (N=1,486) revealed diverse attitudes toward LLMs and substantial noncompliance: 22.5% of conservative-policy reviewers reported using an LLM despite the prohibition, and 36.5% of permissive-policy reviewers reported at least one explicitly disallowed use. We discuss implications for future peer-review policy and tool design.

</details>


### [6] [CARES: A Conversational AI System for Regulation-Grounded Safety Reporting in Construction Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19429)
*Fan Yang,Jiabin Wu,Yuan Tian,Jiansong Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CARES是一个对话式AI系统，通过多智能体对话、检索增强生成与自动报告生成，将法规指导融入建筑施工安全日常报告与教育，初步评估显示其技术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑安全报告多依赖人工记录与静态模板，反馈有限，日常活动与相关法规脱节，缺乏法规知识融入日常文档的手段。

Method: CARES结合主动式多智能体对话、检索增强生成（RAG）与自动报告生成；通过混合检索调取相关法规条文，将对话转为结构化日报，并在对话旁同步展示法规来源与动态报告以支持用户复核修正。

Result: 15名建筑管理学生参与的初步评估考察了检索质量、回答忠实度与对话相关性。CARES总体忠实度得分0.74，回答相关性得分1.00，初始检索排序仍有改进空间。

Conclusion: 结果初步证明法规依循的对话式报告在技术上可行，并凸显将法规知识融入常规文档的机会；未来需评估其对报告质量、安全意识与学习成效的影响。

Abstract: Construction safety reporting often relies on manual logs and static templates that provide limited feedback and leave daily activities disconnected from relevant regulations. This paper introduces CARES (Conversational AI Reporting for Enhanced Safety), a conversational AI system that integrates regulatory guidance into daily reporting to support construction safety education. CARES combines proactive multi-agent dialogue, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and automated report generation. The system guides users through reporting tasks, retrieves relevant regulatory passages using hybrid retrieval, and converts conversations into structured daily reports. Regulatory sources and the evolving report are displayed alongside the dialogue to support user review and correction. A preliminary evaluation involving 15 construction management students assessed retrieval quality, response faithfulness, and conversational relevance. CARES achieved an overall faithfulness score of 0.74 and an answer relevance score of 1.00, while initial retrieval ranking remained an area for improvement. These results provide preliminary evidence of the technical feasibility of regulation-grounded conversational reporting. The study highlights opportunities to integrate regulatory knowledge into routine documentation, with future work needed to evaluate effects on report quality, safety awareness, and learning outcomes.

</details>


### [7] [FenceXR: AR Movement Replay for Error-Detection Training and Spatially Grounded Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19505)
*Avinash Ajit Nargund,Amelia Haruka Harrison,Timothy Robinson,Tobias Höllerer,Misha Sra*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出 FenceXR——一个基于单目手机视频重建 3D 动作回放的增强现实系统，用于击剑训练与反馈，帮助新手识别弓步技术错误，并让教练在具体关节与时刻上附加标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速复杂运动（如击剑）中的技术错误识别是重要的动作学习技能，但新手难以掌握：教练注意力有限、现场示范每次不一致、视频回放受限于录制机位；同时教练与高水平选手在回看时虽能发现问题，却无法将反馈锚定到动作本身，只能用语言描述让学习者自行对应身体。

Method: 开发 FenceXR 增强现实系统，从单目智能手机视频重建 3D 动作回放，包含两个模块：Trainee 模块训练新手识别常见弓步错误；Reviewer 模块允许教练与高水平击剑者将文字或语音标注附加到回放中特定关节与时刻，并可与学员异步共享。开展 18 名新手击剑者研究与 4 名击剑专家的视频研究，结合访谈评估。

Result: 18 名新手在无辅助条件下错误识别准确率从训练前的接近随机水平（37.5%）提升至单次训练后的 64.1%；访谈显示其观察方式从宽泛的视觉扫视转向对特定关节及其时机的针对性检查。4 名击剑专家均认为 Reviewer 模块是其现有教练工具的有价值补充，尤其适用于标准视频难以传达的反馈。

Conclusion: FenceXR 表明基于 AR 的 3D 动作回放能有效提升新手错误识别能力，并支持将反馈锚定于动作本身的关节与时刻；文章最后讨论了将动作训练与反馈根植于动作自身的 AR 系统设计启示。

Abstract: Recognizing technical errors in movement is a perceptual skill important to motor learning, but it is challenging for beginners in fast, complex sports like fencing to develop it. A coach's attention is scarce, live demonstrations vary from repetition to repetition, and video review is limited to whatever camera angle was used to record it. Coaches and advanced fencers reviewing a recording face a related problem. They can see an error, but have no way to anchor their feedback to the movement itself, and are left describing it in words the learner must map back onto their own body. We present FenceXR, an augmented reality system that reconstructs 3D movement replays from monocular smartphone video to address both problems. A Trainee module trains novices to detect common lunge errors while a Reviewer module lets coaches and advanced fencers attach text or voice annotations to a specific joint and moment in a replay, which can be shared asynchronously with a trainee. In a study with 18 novice fencers, unaided error-detection accuracy rose from near-chance (37.5%) before training to 64.1% after a single session, with interviews showing a shift from broad visual scanning toward targeted inspection of specific joints and their timing. In a video-based study with four fencing experts, all four viewed the Reviewer module as a valuable complement to their existing coaching tools, particularly for feedback that is difficult to convey through standard video. We end with a discussion of implications for designing AR systems that ground movement-based training and feedback in the movement itself.

</details>


### [8] [Cyber Exodus: Burnout Symptoms, Exit Intention, and Peer Response in Online Cybersecurity Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19556)
*Nadia Mehjabin,Ji Hyun Kim,Laura Barnes,Koustuv Saha,Henry Kautz,Subigya Nepal*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究将临床倦怠量表改编为文本标注方案，应用于五个网络安全从业者在线社区的约65万条帖子与回复，发现倦怠四症状指向不同的工作问题，其中"心理距离"最关联离职意向，却最少获得同伴的职业建议回应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全从业者倦怠率高、流失严重，而流失本身构成安全问题。但该群体难以研究：安全运营中心对外封闭、通过雇主招募的样本存在偏差、已倦怠者最不可能回答雇主调查。因此需要新的研究途径。

Method: 将经过验证的临床工具"倦怠评估工具（BAT）"改编为文本标注方案，应用于五个网络安全从业者在线社区的354,861条帖子和296,442条回复。由两名受训编码员在100条帖子上进行校验，四症状标注的宏F1为0.75，检测任何倦怠信号的F1为0.98。

Result: 四种倦怠症状指向不同的工作问题，而非同一问题的不同程度。耗竭几乎出现在任何关于人员配置或工作量的抱怨中；心理距离（认为工作不值得的信念丧失）是唯一与运营问题无关的症状，且在仅含单一症状的帖子中，其伴随明确离职意图的频率约为其他症状的两倍。同伴回应呈相反模式：当发帖者表示考虑离职时，回复组合转向职业建议，但这一转变对心理距离最小。

Conclusion: 与离职关联最强的症状（心理距离）恰恰是同伴回应调整最少的一种，而单一倦怠分数会同时掩盖这两种模式。研究提示倦怠应分症状看待，并揭示同伴支持的盲区。

Abstract: Security practitioners burn out at high rates, and the resulting attrition is itself a security problem. This workforce is hard to study: security operations centers are closed to outside researchers, studies that reach practitioners recruit through employers, and those who have disengaged most may have the least reason to answer an employer's survey. The same practitioners discuss their working conditions openly in online communities. We adapt the Burnout Assessment Tool, a validated clinical instrument, into a text annotation scheme and apply it to 354,861 posts and 296,442 replies from five online communities of cybersecurity practitioners. Checked against two trained coders on 100 posts, the annotation reaches a macro F1 of 0.75 across the four symptoms and 0.98 for detecting any burnout signal. We find that the four symptoms point to different problems at work, not to the same problem at different levels of severity. Exhaustion appears in almost any complaint about staffing or workload. Mental distance, a loss of belief that the work is worthwhile, is the only symptom unrelated to operational problems, and among posts with a single symptom it is accompanied by a stated intention to leave roughly twice as often as any other. Peer responses show the opposite pattern. When a poster says they are considering leaving, the mix of replies shifts toward career advice, but this shift is smallest for mental distance. The symptom most strongly associated with leaving is thus the one peers adjust to least, and a single burnout score obscures both patterns.

</details>


### [9] [Learning from Success and Failure: Acquiring Adaptive Dialogue Strategies for Social Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19570)
*Sanae Yamashita,Yuki Okafuji*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究提出利用视觉语言模型（VLM）识别用户属性、并结合对话历史输入大语言模型（LLM）生成对话策略的架构，通过从实地实验互动数据中提取策略并评估，发现显式表示失败策略可补充成功策略并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统社交机器人对话系统需分别依赖对话策略与用户属性识别，两者均需专业知识；真实部署中数据收集成本高，且所得数据集常包含大量失败案例，造成资源浪费。

Method: 提出一种架构：由VLM识别用户属性，将用户属性与对话历史一并输入LLM，生成针对特定用户属性的对话策略；从实地实验收集的交互数据集中提取对话策略并评估其有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，显式表示失败策略能够补充成功策略，并提升整体性能。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一条实用流水线：可从真实部署日志中构建并维护可解释的策略库，将大量失败交互回收为可复用约束，从而降低社交机器人的开发成本。

Abstract: Traditional dialogue systems for social robots require both dialogue strategies and user attribute recognition, each demanding specialized expertise. However, data collection is costly in real-world deployments, and the resulting datasets often include many failure cases. In this study, we aim to automate the acquisition of dialogue strategies by leveraging both successful and failed interactions using a vision-language model (VLM) and a large language model (LLM). We propose an architecture in which user attributes, recognized by the VLM, along with dialogue history, are fed into the LLM to generate dialogue strategies tailored to specific user attributes. We extracted dialogue strategies from an interaction dataset collected through a field experiment and evaluated their effectiveness. The results demonstrate that explicitly representing failure strategies complements success strategies and improves performance. Our findings highlight a practical pipeline for constructing and maintaining an interpretable strategy repository from in-the-wild deployment logs by recycling abundant failure interactions as reusable constraints, ultimately reducing the development cost of social robots.

</details>


### [10] [Value Faces: Surfacing How Self-Presentation Shifts Across Relationships](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19581)
*Gabriel Koo,Rayhan Rashed,Farnaz Jahanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出"展示价值观"概念及Value Faces系统,通过分析日常聊天记录,基于Schwartz十项基本人类价值观为不同关系生成独立的价值观画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们的沟通风格会随关系变化,这种变化也体现在价值观的表达与权衡上,但以往计算研究多关注沟通风格,未捕捉关系特定的价值观表达。

Method: 提出Value Faces系统,分析用户在日常消息平台上已有的聊天历史,采用Schwartz十项基本人类价值观,为不同关系生成各自的价值观画像;通过混合方法研究(N=18)进行评估。

Result: 生成的价值观画像区分参与者关系情境的几率是随机猜测的两倍;系统推断的关系间差异与参与者自身的感知一致。

Conclusion: 参与者借助这些画像表达了此前隐性的自我呈现差异,将其与角色和随时间的变化联系起来,并重新审视了自我评估。

Abstract: People present different aspects of themselves across relationships. Computational work has captured such variation in communication style. But this variation also extends to which principles people foreground or background in a particular relationship--i.e., in the values they express and how they balance them. We conceptualize these relationship-specific expressions of values as demonstrated values. To make demonstrated values visible, we introduce Value Faces, a system that analyzes a person's existing chat histories from their everyday messaging platforms using Schwartz's ten basic human values and produces separate value profiles for their different relationships. In a mixed-methods study(N=18), we find that the resulting value profiles distinguished participants' relational contexts with twice the odds of guessing, while system-inferred differences across relationships aligned with participants' perceptions of those differences. Participants used these profiles to articulate previously implicit differences in how they presented themselves, connect them to roles and changes over time, and reconsider their self-assessments.

</details>


### [11] [DataCanvas-EDU: An Agentic Framework for Instructor-Guided Synthetic Data Generation in Business Analytics Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19617)
*Bang An,Maria Hamdani,Joseph Fox*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出 DataCanvas-EDU，一个用于商业分析教育中教师引导合成数据生成的智能体框架，通过对话式设定教学目标与模式，自动完成数据生成、校验及教辅材料准备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业分析教学需要多样化数据集以适配不同学习目标、学生背景与分析任务，但真实数据获取困难、灵活性低，教师还需投入大量时间探查模式、验证结果并准备作业与参考答案。此外，大语言模型存在训练数据污染问题：常用公开数据集往往附带大量教程与现成分析，模型可能已见过，导致学生只是获得现成解释，而缺乏与 AI 协作探究陌生数据的训练。

Method: 提出 DataCanvas-EDU 智能体框架：教师通过对话说明教学目标与预期模式，AI 智能体编写生成代码、检查生成数据，并准备作业、参考分析与评分量规。整个过程由 Plan、Create、Verify/Test Analysis、Evaluate 四个阶段组织，并支持教师审阅与修改。该框架以可复用的 AI Agent Skill 形式打包，适用于兼容的智能体环境。

Result: 以 WindowDash 食品配送案例进行演示，该案例包含 15,000 条订单与九个设计好的模式，展示框架在案例生成与教辅材料准备上的可行性与效果。

Conclusion: DataCanvas-EDU 有望简化教学案例准备流程，同时为学生创造与 AI 协作探究新设计模式的机会。工具包与安装说明已开源于 https://github.com/BANG23333/datacanvas-edu。

Abstract: Business analytics education requires diverse datasets to support different learning objectives, student backgrounds, and analytical tasks. Real-world data can be difficult to obtain and offer limited flexibility for adapting a case to a particular course. Even when suitable data are available, instructors must investigate the patterns, verify the results, and prepare assignments and reference solutions, requiring substantial time and effort. The use of large language models (LLMs) introduces an additional concern about training data contamination. Widely used public datasets often have extensive tutorials and worked analyses that models may have encountered during training. Students may therefore receive explanations drawn from existing analyses without practicing how to investigate unfamiliar data in collaboration with AI. This paper presents DataCanvas-EDU, an agentic framework for instructor-guided synthetic data generation in business analytics education. Instructors specify teaching goals and intended patterns through conversation, while an AI agent writes generation code, checks the resulting data, and prepares assignments, reference analyses, and rubrics. Four phases, Plan, Create, Verify / Test Analysis, and Evaluate, organize the process and support instructor review and revision. The framework is intended to simplify case preparation while creating opportunities for students to investigate newly designed patterns with AI. We illustrate the approach with WindowDash, a food delivery case containing 15,000 orders and nine designed patterns. DataCanvas-EDU is packaged as a reusable AI Agent Skill for compatible agent environments, with the package and installation instructions available at https://github.com/BANG23333/datacanvas-edu

</details>


### [12] [Faithful Where It Can Be Checked: Auditing a Reflection Agent Against Its System Prompt in a Randomized Trial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19635)
*Subigya K. Nepal,Serena Soh,Noah Vinoya,SoHyun Park,Mahnaz Roshanaei,Gabriella Harari*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对17,930轮对话进行编码并与随机试验调查数据关联，发现AI反思智能体倾向遵守易核查的规则（如回复长度上限），却违反"勿奉承""温和质疑"等难核查指令，且"要求做出决定"的行为与被试职业规划承诺度下降、疑虑增加相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话智能体被日益用于引导反思，但一项随机试验显示GPT-4o职业反思智能体的参与者职业规划承诺度更低、更怀疑，因此需要弄清智能体的具体行为如何导致这一较差结果，并为反思智能体设计及可核查指令的撰写提供依据。

Method: 对两项研究中的全部17,930轮对话进行编码，将编码结果与人工编码者对照校验，并将对话与试验调查数据关联，分析智能体实际遵循与违反指令的行为模式。

Result: 智能体只遵循易于核查的规则（如回复长度上限）；被要求不要奉承却在一半的回合中赞美参与者；被要求温和质疑却几乎从不质疑，且此类违规不留可见痕迹。与较差结果最相关的行为是"要求做出决定"：调查只问一次决定，而智能体在参与者犹豫时反复追问，被追问最多者最终最为怀疑。

Conclusion: 研究结果为反思智能体的设计以及可核查指令的撰写提供了启示：指令应兼具可核查性，并需关注智能体在用户犹豫时的追问行为。

Abstract: Conversational agents are increasingly used to guide reflection. A recent randomized trial compared a GPT-4o career reflection agent with the same program in a static journaling survey. Agent participants ended less committed to their career plans and more doubtful. We coded all 17,930 turns from its two studies, checked our coding against human coders and linked conversations to the trial's surveys. The rules the agent followed were the easy-to-check ones, like a reply length cap. Told not to flatter, it praised participants in half of its turns; told to challenge gently, it almost never did, and such a break leaves no visible trace. The behavior tied to the worse outcome was the demand to decide: the survey posed each decision once, while the agent asked again when participants hesitated, and those pressed most ended most doubtful. Our findings inform reflection agent design and the writing of checkable instructions.

</details>


### [13] [Treadstone: A Social-Media-Inspired Platform for Multi-Agent Collaborative Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19774)
*Hyunwook Lee,Sungbeom Cho,William Benjamin,Changhee Lee,Hyotaek Jeon,Daeun Jeong,Sungbok Shin,Sungahn Ko,Niklas Elmqvist*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"智能体社会数据分析"范式，通过借鉴社交媒体时间线的共享协调信息流，让人类分析师与AI智能体以异步、线程化的方式协作分析数据，并在TREADSTONE平台实现与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类分析师与自主AI智能体协作面临与人人协作相同的挑战：共享中间结果、避免冲突、维持群体意识。现有工具依赖非结构化消息或单线程聊天机器人交互，缺乏追踪演进假设或关联主张与证据的结构。

Method: 提出智能体社会数据分析范式，将社会数据分析扩展为以社交媒体内容时间线为模型的共享协调信息流；在TREADSTONE平台实例化，人类与AI智能体通过共享信息流中的线程化消息异步发布、链接和争论分析主张，智能体可主动广播假设，用户通过轻量级策展引导分析。

Result: 定性用户研究表明，TREADSTONE促进了协作并保留了人类的分析能动性，与常规聊天机器人交互的孤立体验形成对比。

Conclusion: 基于共享协调信息流的智能体社会数据分析，能够在机器自主性与人类分析控制之间取得平衡，为人类—AI协作分析提供可行范式。

Abstract: Coordinating human analysts with autonomous AI agents faces the same challenges as human-to-human collaboration: sharing intermediate results, avoiding conflicts, and maintaining group awareness. Current tools rely on unstructured messaging or single-threaded chatbot interaction, which lack the structure to track evolving hypotheses or link claims to evidence. We propose agentic social data analysis, a collaboration paradigm extending social data analysis with a shared coordination feed modeled on the content timeline in social media services. We instantiate this concept in TREADSTONE, a platform where human and AI agents asynchronously post, link, and contest analytical claims via threaded messages within a shared feed. By allowing agents to proactively broadcast hypotheses and enabling users to steer the analysis through lightweight curation, Treadstone seeks to balance machine autonomy with human analytical control. A qualitative user study shows that Treadstone fosters collaboration while preserving human analytical agency, in contrast to the solitary experience of conventional chatbot interaction.

</details>


### [14] [Point, Revise, Review: Grounded Agentic Analysis in Reactive Notebooks with marimo-lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19839)
*Péter Ferenc Gyarmati,Trevor Manz,Dominik Moritz,Mennatallah El-Assady*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: marimo-lens 是一个为响应式 Python notebook marimo 设计的扩展，用于实现有根有据的人机协作分析。它将人类标记的输出和注释与其生成单元格及相关计算连接起来，为智能体提供计算背景，并呈现智能体活动、返回结果、保留人类选择，从而弥合人机之间的情境脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算笔记本中，人类与智能体之间存在情境脱节：人类指向渲染结果问"这个"，而智能体通过单元格、依赖关系和运行时状态操作。当人类所见与智能体所操作的内容断开时，人类只能以散文描述意图，失去了视觉和计算情境。该研究旨在解决这一鸿沟，实现有根有据的人机分析。

Method: 提出 marimo-lens，作为响应式 Python 笔记本 marimo 的扩展。它将人类的标记输出和注释连接到其生成单元格及相关贡献计算，为智能体请求提供计算上下文；同时呈现智能体活动，将选定结果返回笔记本，并保留初始选择以供人类审查和重新打开。

Result: 通过对一个真实开放数据集进行探索性人机分析，展示了完整生命周期：视觉情境化的提问引导出计算检查、笔记本操作，并返回证据供人类审查。

Conclusion: marimo-lens 成功连接了人类视觉情境与智能体计算操作，支持有根有据的人机协作分析，提供了从视觉提问到计算检查再到证据返回的完整闭环。

Abstract: In a computational notebook, a human can point to a rendered result and ask about "this," while an agent acts through cells, dependencies, and runtime state. When what the human sees and what the agent operates on are disconnected, the human must describe what they mean and trace what the agent did in prose that strips away situated visual and computational context. We present marimo-lens, an extension to the reactive Python notebook marimo for grounded human-agent analysis. Lens connects a human's marked output and note to its producing cell and relevant contributing computation, giving the agent computational context for the request. It surfaces agent activity, returns selected results to the notebook, and preserves the initiating selection for human review and reopening. We illustrate the lifecycle through an exploratory human-agent analysis of a real-world open dataset, showing how visually situated questions lead to computational inspection, notebook action, and returned evidence for human review.

</details>


### [15] [Penquiry: A Pen-based Interactive In-situ Q&A System Leveraging LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19870)
*Jeongmin Rhee,Changhee Lee,Hyunwoo Kim,Kiroong Choe,Bohyoung Kim,Sungahn Ko,Jinwook Seo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Penquiry 是一个基于笔的原地问答系统，通过在数字学习材料上直接用笔提问，弥合笔式工作流与 LLM 键盘交互之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 笔式数字设备是主动、认知投入型学习的首选媒介，而 LLM 已成为自主学习的重要工具。然而，笔式工作流的流畅、空间化特性与 LLM 离散、重键盘的输入要求之间存在根本性的交互鸿沟。

Method: 作者刻画了两种多模态转换中的交互挑战：指称障碍（难以将细粒度视觉元素锚定到查询上下文）与表达障碍（需将方程、图表等非文本意图转译为僵硬的打字句子）。为此，Penquiry 引入中介层，包含 Content Snapping（内容吸附）以实现无歧义指称，以及 Question Autocompletion（问题自动补全）将稀疏的墨迹关键词扩展为丰富语义查询。

Result: 通过两项迭代用户研究（每项 N=16），Penquiry 相比传统界面显著降低了提问的认知与体力开销。

Conclusion: Penquiry 为基于笔的原地 AI 交互提供了新蓝图。

Abstract: Pen-based digital devices remain a preferred medium for active, cognitively engaging study. Concurrently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have become indispensable for self-directed learning, enabling students to clarify concepts. However, a fundamental interaction gap exists between the fluid, spatial nature of pen-based workflows and the discrete, keyboard-heavy requirements of LLMs. We present Penquiry, an in-situ question-and-answer system that bridges this gap by enabling learners to pose questions directly on digital study materials via a pen. We characterize two primary interaction challenges in this multimodal transition: a Referential Barrier, which hinders grounding fine-grained visual elements into the query context, and an Expressive Barrier, which forces learners to translate diverse, non-textual intents--such as equations and diagrams--into rigid, typed sentences. To resolve these, Penquiry introduces a mediation layer featuring Content Snapping for unambiguous referencing and Question Autocompletion to expand sparse ink keywords into rich semantic queries. Through two iterative user studies (N = 16 per study), we demonstrate that Penquiry significantly reduces the cognitive and physical overhead of inquiry compared to traditional interfaces, providing a new blueprint for pen-based, in-situ AI interaction

</details>


### [16] [What People Almost Did: Evaluating LLM Social Simulations Beyond Behavioral Fit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20055)
*JaeWon Kim,Angie Boggust*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"表征充分性"作为LLM社会模拟的新评估目标，关注模拟是否保留行为背后的推理过程，而非仅行为拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM社会模拟主要评估"行为拟合"，即智能体是否复现所模拟人群的行动或响应分布。但模拟的价值不止于行为拟合，还包括解释人类行为、诊断障碍、比较大规模干预。这些用途依赖理解人们"为什么"这样行动，而不仅是"做了什么"。行为拟合不足，因为行为无法唯一确定其背后的推理过程：例如沉默可能源于不感兴趣，也可能源于言论被压制；不接电话可能是不信任来电者，也可能是缺少电话。

Method: 提出"表征充分性"（representational adequacy）作为LLM社会模拟的新评估目标。利用LLM的推理轨迹（reasoning traces），衡量模拟的"场景—推理—行动"三元组是否以一种忠实于所模拟人群与场景的方式，保留了行为背后的推理过程。同时区分表征充分性与可解释性、对齐性指标，并提出将其整合进模拟研究的方式，将其实测呈现为开放问题。

Result: 本文为立场/概念性论文，未报告实证结果，主要贡献是提出表征充分性概念、与既有指标的区分，以及测量方法作为开放问题的提出。

Conclusion: 行为拟合不足以支撑解释、诊断和干预比较类主张，因为行为欠定推理过程。研究者应以表征充分性作为新评估目标，通过场景—推理—行动三元组检验推理过程的保真性，其测量仍为待解决的开放问题。

Abstract: LLM-based social simulations are primarily evaluated for behavioral fit, testing whether agents reproduce the actions or response distributions of the people they are simulating. However, the promise of simulation extends beyond behavioral fit. Simulations can explain human behavior, diagnose barriers, and compare large-scale interventions. These use cases depend on understanding \textit{why} people acted a certain way, not just \textit{what} they did. As a result, behavioral fit is insufficient for these types of claims because behavior underdetermines the reasoning process behind it. For instance, the behavior of staying silent may be due to disinterest or suppressed speech, and not answering a call may be due to distrust of the caller or limited phone access. In this paper, we propose \textit{representational adequacy} as a new evaluation target for LLM-based social simulations. By leveraging LLM reasoning traces, representational adequacy measures whether a simulation's scenario--reasoning--action triples preserve the reasoning process behind the behavior in a way that is faithful to the population and scenarios being simulated. We distinguish representational adequacy from interpretability and alignment metrics, propose ways to integrate it into simulation research, and pose its measurement as an open problem.

</details>


### [17] [AI Should Facilitate Democratic Deliberation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20059)
*José Ramón Enríquez,Jiaxin Pei,Alex Pentland*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文主张AI辅助协商（而非委托式民主）是强化民主的更优路径，通过降低参与门槛、保留人类自主权来支持大规模协商，并提出四项指导原则。同时指出对齐、谄媚、训练偏见与过度依赖等挑战，呼吁机器学习社区以"能否促进知情、代表性、抗摩擦的话语"为评估标准来开发协商型AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有民主机制面临认知、社会、平台设计与市场驱动等多重摩擦，导致有意义的公众参与受阻。相较于"流动民主"等通过投票委托重构代表制的方案，作者希望找到一种不替代人类判断、仅降低参与门槛的路径，因此提出以AI辅助协商作为强化民主的方向。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）形式，结合在线协商平台的证据与实验研究，构建论证框架；对比"流动民主"式的代表制重构与"AI辅助协商"路径，提炼出四项指导原则，并梳理关键挑战。

Result: 提出四項指导原则：保留能动性与自主性、鼓励相互尊重、促进平等与包容、增强而非替代积极的公民参与。同时识别出四项关键挑战：对齐（alignment）、谄媚（sycophancy）、训练偏见、对AI系统的过度依赖。

Conclusion: AI辅助协商优于重构代表制的方案，能在不替代人类选择的前提下降低参与壁垒。作者呼吁机器学习社区开发以协商为核心的AI系统，并以能否促进知情、具代表性、抗摩擦的话语作为评估标准，而非以参与度指标衡量。

Abstract: AI systems can strengthen democracy by supporting deliberation at scale by addressing cognitive, social, platform-design, and market-driven frictions, while preserving human agency. Unlike proposals such as liquid democracy that restructure representation through vote delegation, in this position paper, we argue that AI-assisted deliberation offers a more promising path by lowering barriers to meaningful engagement without substituting machine judgment for human choice. Drawing on evidence from online deliberation platforms and experimental research, we identify four guiding principles: preserving agency and autonomy, encouraging mutual respect, promoting equality and inclusiveness, and augmenting rather than substituting active citizenship. We also address critical challenges, including alignment, sycophancy, training bias, and over-reliance on AI systems. We call on the machine learning community to develop deliberation-focused AI systems evaluated not on engagement metrics but on their capacity to facilitate informed, representative, and friction-robust discourse.

</details>


### [18] [Designing Against Deskilling: Metacognitive Feedback Reduces Cognitive Offloading to LLM Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20143)
*Sebastian Maier,Kai Schwabe,Manuel Schneider,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过预注册在线实验（N=704）检验两种干预（元认知反馈与基于努力的奖励）对减少AI认知卸载与技能退化的作用，发现元认知反馈有效，奖励无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将认知任务卸载给AI会减少技能练习机会，带来"去技能化"（deskilling）风险。然而，如何在不限制AI使用的前提下防止去技能化仍不清楚。

Method: 设计两种干预：（1）元认知反馈，向用户明确揭示卸载行为对自身的影响；（2）基于努力的奖励，激励用户减少对LLM的广泛依赖。采用2×2设计并设置无AI对照组，进行预注册在线实验（N=704）。任务为借助基于LLM的助手练习分数算术，助手仅在用户明确请求时提供解答，随后进行无辅助测试。

Result: 元认知反馈减少了答案卸载（OR=0.47），并提升了测试表现（OR=1.51）。未发现证据表明奖励对任一结果有影响。

Conclusion: 元认知反馈是一种有前景的设计选择，可有效减少认知卸载，在不限制AI使用的前提下缓解去技能化风险。

Abstract: Cognitive offloading to AI can reduce opportunities to practice skills, creating risks of deskilling. However, it remains unclear how to prevent deskilling without restricting access to AI. Here, we design two interventions to reduce offloading decisions: (1) metacognitive feedback that makes the implications of offloading for users explicit, and (2) an effort-based reward that incentivizes less extensive LLM assistance. We test both in a preregistered online experiment ($N = 704$) with a 2$\times$2 design and a no-AI control. The task was to practice fraction arithmetic with an LLM-based assistant that provided solutions only on explicit request, followed by an unaided test. Metacognitive feedback reduced answer offloading (OR $= 0.47$) and improved test performance (OR $= 1.51$). We found no evidence that the reward affected either outcome. Our results identify metacognitive feedback as a promising design choice to reduce cognitive offloading.

</details>


### [19] [A Hybrid Gaze-Motor Imagery BCI Framework for Effective Decision Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20273)
*Gowtham Reddy N,KongFatt Wong-Lin,Yogesh Kumar Meena*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探究视觉注视对运动想象神经响应稳定性的影响，并提出一种结合眼动追踪与运动想象的新型异步混合脑机接口范式，显著提升准确率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非侵入式脑机接口与眼动追踪技术为通信提供了新途径，但基于运动想象（MI）的脑机接口存在判别性低、受试者间差异大的问题，限制其实际应用。

Method: 研究视觉注视对独立MI及MI-眼动混合系统中神经响应稳定性的影响，提出一种异步混合范式：先用眼动追踪直接选择目标，再用MI进行确认，减少操作步骤。15名健康受试者，使用16通道EEG系统进行评估，并对比有限通道与全导联配置。

Result: MI相关信息主要定位于运动皮层区域；有限通道配置（SVM：0.58）性能与全导联配置（SVM：0.54）相当；混合MI范式优于传统MI，最高达100%准确率，且在各类通道配置下鲁棒性更强。

Conclusion: 视觉注视可增强神经响应稳定性，将眼动追踪与MI结合能够构建可靠、可扩展的多指令脑机接口系统，适用于真实场景应用。

Abstract: Non-invasive brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) and eye-tracking technologies offer promising communication pathways; however, motor imagery (MI)-based BCIs often suffer from low discriminability and high inter-subject variability. To mitigate these issues, this study investigates the impact of visual fixation on neural response stability in both standalone MI and hybrid MI-eye tracking systems. We then propose a novel asynchronous hybrid paradigm that streamlines user intent by utilising eye-tracking for direct selection, followed by MI-based confirmation, significantly reducing the operational steps required by conventional systems. The paradigm was evaluated with 15 healthy participants using a 16-channel EEG system. Results show that MI-related information is predominantly localised within motor cortex regions, with limited-channel configurations (SVM: 0.58) achieving performance comparable to full-montage setups (SVM: 0.54). The hybrid MI paradigm further outperforms conventional MI, achieving up to 100% accuracy with greater robustness across all channel configurations. Our findings indicate that visual fixation enhances neural response stability, while integrating eye-tracking with MI enables the development of reliable, scalable multi-command BCI systems suitable for real-world applications.

</details>


### [20] [A Multi-Objective Optimisation Framework for Corticomuscular EEG-EMG Pair Selection in Hybrid BCI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20275)
*Dekka Muni Kumar,Yogesh Kumar Meena*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的EEG-EMG通道对选择框架，将通道对选择建模为约束双目标优化问题并用NSGA-II求解，在八名卒中患者的运动想象数据上取得89.6%的平均分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 融合EEG与EMG的混合脑机接口在提升运动想象分类可靠性方面潜力显著，但如何识别能有效捕捉皮质-肌肉交互的有信息通道对仍是难题。现有方法多依赖人工预设的通道组合，跨被试泛化能力差。

Method: 将通道对选择构建为受约束的双目标优化问题，同时最大化EEG通道相对运动皮层区域的空间相关性以及EEG与EMG信号间的皮质-肌肉耦合强度，采用NSGA-II自动搜索最优通道对子集。特征提取方面，将刻画EEG-EMG交互的带功率时间特征相关性分析与基于ERD的EEG特征相结合，并引入滑动窗口的时序分析方法，以适应运动想象信号的动态特性。

Result: 在八名卒中患者的运动想象数据上进行评估，平均分类准确率达到89.6%。

Conclusion: 该框架能够捕捉具有生理意义的皮质-肌肉交互，并有效提升运动想象分类性能，为混合脑机接口的通道对自动选择提供了可行的数据驱动方案。

Abstract: Hybrid brain-computer interface (BCI) systems that integrate electroencephalography (EEG) and electromyography (EMG) signals have shown significant potential in improving the reliability of motor imagery (MI) classification, particularly in neuro-rehabilitation applications. However, identifying informative EEG-EMG channel pairs that effectively capture corticomuscular interactions remains a challenging problem, as existing approaches typically rely on manually predefined channel combinations that may not generalise across subjects. In this work, a data-driven EEG-EMG pair selection framework is proposed, in which channel pair selection is formulated as a constrained bi-objective optimisation problem. The proposed method jointly maximises the spatial relevance of EEG channels with respect to motor cortex regions and the corticomuscular coupling strength between EEG and EMG signals, and is solved using the NSGA-II to automatically identify an optimal subset of pairs. To extract discriminative features, the correlation between band-power time features capturing EEG-EMG interaction is combined with ERD-based EEG features, and a sliding-window-based temporal analysis is employed to account for the dynamic nature of MI signals. The proposed framework is evaluated on MI data from eight stroke patients and achieves an average classification accuracy of 89.6%, demonstrating its effectiveness in capturing physiologically meaningful corticomuscular interactions and improving classification performance.

</details>


### [21] [How Do We Visualize Space in Molecular Biology? A Study of Spatial Transcriptomics Visualization Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20324)
*Denisse Chacón-Ramírez,Mark S. Keller,Eric Mörth,Nils Gehlenborg,Marc Streit,Andreas Hinterreiter*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 一篇综述分析了148篇空间转录组学论文和1,824张图表面板，用"What-Why-How"编码框架（基于Munzner嵌套模型）梳理可视化中的数据表示、生物学任务与设计选择，并评估交互式可视化工具的能力，最后总结现状与未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞身份取决于其在组织中的空间位置（如巨噬细胞在肿瘤核心与边缘表现不同）。空间转录组学虽能恢复空间坐标，但产生高维、多模态且不确定的数据，将这三者同时可视化本身就是一个难题。因此有必要评估该领域目前如何呈现这些数据、哪些方法有效、哪些仍然缺失。

Method: 综述性研究，采用"What-Why-How"编码框架，基于Munzner的嵌套模型。系统调研了148篇论文和1,824张图表面板，将所表示的数据、驱动每次可视化的生物学任务、以及体现这些任务的设计选择三者联系起来。此外，还对部分调研工作中提供的专门交互式可视化软件进行了考察，分析其支持的交互能力（这些能力不一定反映在静态图表中）。

Result: 对空间转录组学可视化的文献现状进行了系统性的编码与分类，明确了数据表示、生物学任务与可视化设计选择之间的关系，并额外梳理了相关交互式可视化软件所支持的交互能力。

Conclusion: 总结了该领域当前的状况，并指出了未来生物信息学与可视化研究者需要共同面对的挑战。

Abstract: A cell's identity depends on where it sits in tissue: for example, a macrophage behaves differently in a tumor core than at its edge. Spatial transcriptomics has transformed how we study this by recovering that lost coordinate, but it does so by producing data that is simultaneously high-dimensional, multimodal, and uncertain. Visualizing this combination is a hard problem in its own right, and one that warrants an assessment of how the field currently represents it, what has worked, and what is still missing. We surveyed 148 papers and 1,824 figure panels using a What-Why-How coding framework grounded in Munzner's nested model, connecting the data represented, the biological tasks motivating each visualization, and the design choices through which they are expressed; a subset of the surveyed work also contributed dedicated interactive visualization software that was not necessarily reflected in the static figures, and we looked at what interaction capabilities those tools supported as well. We close by outlining where the field stands and the challenges ahead for bioinformatics and visualization researchers to tackle together.

</details>


### [22] [TeamCAMS: An Open-Source Research Platform for Studying Human Behaviour in Human-AI Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20490)
*Amos Brocco,Alain Chavaillaz,Andreas Sonderegger,Juergen Sauer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍TeamCAMS（座舱空气管理系统），一个用于模拟人-AI交互的协作研究环境，基于多项心理学理论开发，采用开源许可向研究社区开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为科学研究提供模拟人机（人-AI）交互的协作工作环境，支持多任务过程控制情境下的实证研究，并推动透明度、可复现性与后续开发。

Method: 构建多任务仿真系统TeamCAMS，建模过程控制环境；以多项心理学理论指导其开发；在既往版本基础上扩展新版本功能，支持团队协作与人-AI交互相关研究问题。

Result: 早期版本已用于实证研究，涉及比较不同自动化形式、评估自动化可靠性的影响、压力对多任务绩效的影响等；最新版本可支持团队冲突、分布式团队协作及人-AI交互等新研究问题；未来还可扩展至领导自动化等新领域。

Conclusion: TeamCAMS可作为开源工具供研究社区使用，促进人-AI交互与团队协作研究的透明性、可复现性和进一步发展。

Abstract: In this article, we present TeamCAMS (Cabin Air Management System), a collaborative work environment for simulating human-AI (artificial intelligence) interaction for scientific research. The article outlines how several psychological theories guided the development of this multiple-task simulation. Modelling a process control environment, previous versions of TeamCAMS have already been used in empirical studies to address a wide range of research questions (e.g., comparing different forms of automation, evaluating impact of automation reliability, effects of stress on multiple-task performance). Outlining the technical possibilities offered by TeamCAMS, the article points out how its latest version offers researchers the possibility of addressing a set of new research questions including problems associated with teamwork (e.g., within-team conflict, distributed teamwork) and human-AI interaction. Finally, we will outline how this simulation environment can be enhanced further still to address research questions in new fields (e.g., automation of leadership). To promote transparency, reproducibility, and further development, TeamCAMS is made available to the research community under an open-source license.

</details>


### [23] [Stereotypically Yours: Portrayal and Perception of Race-Coded AI Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20637)
*Wang Claire,Jiayue Melissa Shi,Agam Goyal,Grace Sletten,Renwen Zhang,Eshwar Chandrasekharan,Koustuv Saha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究结合算法审计与用户访谈，考察种族化AI伴侣如何表现身份及其用户如何解读，揭示种族刻板印象的系统性差异与用户期待的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI伴侣可能采用种族人格，引发对其身份表征方式及用户解读的疑问，需探究个性化设计是否在无意中复制刻板印象。

Method: 对种族编码的AI人格进行算法审计，并对12名使用探测工具的伴侣用户进行访谈，再将结果与理论三角互证。

Result: 审计发现系统性差异，如开放权重模型中亚裔编码男性人格的顺从得分高于白人，黑人、西班牙裔和原住民男性人格的攻击性得分高于白人；访谈显示用户既期待文化熟悉感与外部视角，又在何种呈现有意义或属刻板印象上存在分歧，部分人排斥显性种族信号却仍期待文化差异回应。

Conclusion: 社会规范与文化期待使有意义的种族表征难以避免复制刻板印象，伴侣个性化评估应超越用户满意度，纳入更广泛的表征性伤害考量。

Abstract: AI companions can purportedly adopt racial personas, raising questions about how they represent identity and how users interpret these portrayals. We combined an algorithmic audit of race-coded AI personas with interviews with 12 companion users who interacted with a probe. Our audit revealed systematic differences, such as Asian-coded male personas receiving higher submissiveness scores than White counterparts, and Black, Hispanic, and Indigenous male personas receiving higher aggression scores than their White counterparts in open-weight models. Interviews revealed that participants envisioned AI companions as offering cultural familiarity and outside perspectives, but differed in which portrayals they considered meaningful or stereotypical. Some rejected overt racial signaling while still expecting culturally distinctive responses. Triangulating these findings with theory, we highlight how social norms and cultural expectations complicate efforts to support meaningful racial representation without reproducing stereotypes. We discuss how companion personalization should be evaluated beyond user satisfaction to account for broader representational harms.

</details>


### [24] [Ageing, Digital Literacy, and Interaction Modality in Immer-sive Virtual Reality: Psychomotor Performance, Cognitive Flexibility, and Their Processing-Speed Association](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20719)
*Panagiotis Kourtesis,Katerina Denaxa,Lydia Asimakopoulo,Petros Roussos,Maria Roussou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究考察年龄、数字素养与交互模态对沉浸式VR中精神运动和认知灵活性表现的影响，发现年龄相关减速普遍存在，控制器直接触摸的相对年龄梯度最陡但绝对速度仍快；数字素养仅与TMT-VR完成速度相关，Fitts速度分数可部分解释年龄与TMT-VR表现的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR日益用于训练与认知评估，但其交互技术的年龄敏感性尚不明确，需要厘清年龄、数字素养和交互模态如何影响表现，以指导面向全年龄段的评估设计。

Method: 202名19-90岁成人完成五种模式（眼动注视、头部注视、控制器射线投射、虚拟手指、控制器直接触摸）的Fitts定律任务、VR版连线测验（TMT-VR）及数字素养问卷，并进行分析。

Result: 年龄与所有模式任务时间变慢相关；控制器直接触摸的相对年龄梯度最陡，但绝对速度仍居前列，说明技术改变相对年龄敏感性却不决定绝对效率。数字素养高者TMT-VR完成更快，但与Fitts任务时间无关。Fitts速度分数在年龄之外预测TMT-VR表现，并部分解释其年龄关联，提示共享加工速度方差；探索性全电池分析将该关联限于完成时间和误差校正时间指标，数字素养关联限于误差校正时间指标。

Conclusion: 面向全年龄的XR评估应标准化交互模态，评估而非排除空中直接选择技术，并结合数字素养与精神运动速度解释认知分数。

Abstract: Extended reality (XR) increasingly supports training and cognitive assessment, yet the age sensitivity of its interaction techniques is unclear. This study examined age, digital literacy, and interaction modality as correlates of psychomotor and cognitive-flexibility performance in immersive virtual reality (VR). Two hundred and two adults (19-90 years) completed a five-mode Fitts' law task (eye-gaze, head-gaze, controller ray-casting, virtual finger, and controller direct touch), the Trail Making Test in VR (TMT-VR), and a digital-literacy questionnaire. Age was associated with slower task times across modes, but controller direct touch carried the steepest relative age gradient yet remained among the fastest in absolute terms; technique altered relative age sensitivity without determining absolute efficiency. Higher digital literacy was associated with faster TMT-VR completion but not Fitts task times. A Fitts-derived speed score predicted TMT-VR performance beyond age and partly accounted for its age association, consistent with shared processing-speed variance; an exploratory full-battery extension confined the Fitts-score association to completion-time and error-adjusted-time indices and the digital-literacy association to error-adjusted-time indices, not wrong-target errors, mean selection distance, or relative Part B indices. Age-inclusive XR assessment should standardise interaction modality, evaluate rather than exclude mid-air direct selection, and interpret cognitive scores alongside digital literacy and psychomotor speed.

</details>


### [25] [What Parents Can See: Divergent Accounts of Youth AI Companion Use in Parenting and Teenager Subreddits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20720)
*Thomas Berkane,Anne Bischops,Anika Mellacheruvu,Maimuna Majumder*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过分析1,628条Reddit帖子并结合LLM进行大规模编码，研究发现青少年与家长对AI伴侣使用的描述存在系统性差异：青少年最常谈情感支持，家长最常谈浪漫/性互动；差异源于不同使用方式对外部观察者的可见性不同，因此家长与系统设计应关注那些"既不留痕迹、也不显眼"的使用场景，尤其是情感支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青少年日益使用AI伴侣，而家长是主要的中介者，其引导是否有效取决于家长是否了解青少年的真实使用方式及其风险与收益。然而既有研究将青少年与家长两个群体分开考察，且现有分类体系几乎只关注风险，忽视了收益与其他使用模式，导致对家长认知偏差缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 从育儿社区与青少年社区收集2023—2026年间共1,628条关于青少年使用AI伴侣的Reddit帖子；构建涵盖使用模式、风险、收益与家长中介行为的编码手册；并借助LLM在语料库规模上应用该编码手册进行编码与对比分析。作者进一步提出"可见性"解释框架，说明不同使用方式如何被外部观察者察觉。

Result: 两个社区呈现显著分歧：青少年最常讨论从AI伴侣获得情感支持（青少年帖31% vs 家长帖19%）；家长最常讨论青少年将AI伴侣用于浪漫与性互动（36% vs 25%）。青少年并非忽视风险，依恋与依赖是他们提及最多的风险（19%），与家长比例接近（16%）。青少年还描述了以风险为中心的分类体系未能捕捉、且家长很少提及的收益，最突出的是情感支持（27% vs 5%）。作者认为这些差异与不同使用方式对外部观察者的可见性有关：整夜长聊会留下聊天之外的痕迹，露骨性内容在被查看记录时格外显眼，而倾诉朋友间争吵则两者都不具备。

Conclusion: 青少年与家长叙述的差异可归因于使用方式的"可见性"：依赖类使用会外溢留下痕迹，性内容在日志中显眼，而情感支持类使用既不留痕迹也不显眼，因此最容易被家长忽视。家长引导与系统设计应尤其关注这类"两条路径都无法到达家长"的使用场景，其中情感支持最为首要。

Abstract: Youth increasingly use AI companions, and parents are the primary mediators of that use. How effective that mediation can be depends on whether parents are aware of how adolescents actually use these systems and what risks and benefits such use carries; nevertheless, prior work has only studied these demographic groups in isolation, and existing taxonomies attend almost entirely to risk. We analyze 1,628 Reddit posts about youth AI companion use from parenting and teenager communities (2023--2026); develop a codebook covering modes of use, risks, benefits, and parental mediation; and apply it at corpus scale with an LLM. The two communities yield divergent accounts. Teenagers most often discuss receipt of emotional support from AI companions (31% of teenager posts vs. 19% of parenting posts), whereas parents most often discuss teenage use of AI companions for romantic and sexual interaction (36% vs. 25%). Teenagers are not unaware of other risks, however; indeed, attachment and dependence is the risk they raise most (19%), close to the parental rate (16%). Teenagers also describe benefits that risk-centered taxonomies do not capture and parents rarely mention, most notably emotional support (27% vs. 5%). We argue these differences track what a given kind of use makes visible to someone outside the conversation. Chatting with a companion for hours every night leaves a trace beyond the chat itself; sexting with a character stands out when a parent reads the log; venting about a fight with a friend does neither, since it looks like any other conversation. The first surfaces as dependence, the second as sexual content, and the third as emotional support, which is the one parents most often miss. Parental guidance and system design should attend to use cases that reach parents by neither route, emotional support foremost among them.

</details>


### [26] [Semantic Action Graph: A Shared Representation for Agent Grounding and Human Interpretation of Sports Highlights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20768)
*Tica Lin,Deepak Chandran,Gauri Jagatap,Chen Chen,Andrea Fanelli,David Gunawan,Josh Kimball*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"语义动作图"（semantic action graph），一种轻量级领域模式，用来将体育比赛表示为表演者、动作、接受者、时刻和状态节点，并通过角色、时间和结果边连接，从而同时支撑智能体生成解说集锦与观众的交互式查询与校验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式智能体在选取与解说视频集锦时，通常基于非结构化或帧级表示，导致输出难以被观众验证，也难以按个人偏好进行调控。作者希望找到一种既能为智能体生成提供基础，又能让人类理解和操作的统一表示。

Method: 作者设计了语义动作图这一轻量级领域模式，具备三个关键特性：1）连贯的事件序列；2）共享的封闭词汇表；3）可帧定位的时刻。该模式同时服务于两类使用者：负责组合解说集锦的智能体流水线，以及让观众查询和检查同一结构的可视化界面。作者将其实例化为 SportSAGE——一个配对四模块集锦流水线与图界面的设计探针，并收集了 12 位足球迷的反馈。

Result: 参与者对生成集锦和解说的质量表示满意，并使用图界面来搜索、浏览和解读比赛集锦。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，一个小的、人类可读的模式可以同时为智能体生成提供依据并支持人类解读。

Abstract: Generative agents are increasingly used to select and narrate video highlights, but they typically operate over unstructured or frame-level representations. Their output is consequently difficult for a viewer to verify and steer toward individual preferences. We present the semantic action graph, a lightweight domain schema that represents a sports match as performer, action, recipient, moment, and state nodes connected by role, temporal, and outcome edges. The schema demonstrates three key properties: 1) connected event sequences, 2) a shared, closed vocabulary, and 3) frame-addressable moments, making it suitable to serve two consumers at once: an agentic pipeline that composes narrated highlights, and a visual interface through which viewers query and inspect the same structure. We instantiate it in SportSAGE, a design probe pairing a four-module highlight pipeline with a graph interface, and report feedback from 12 soccer fans. Participants were satisfied with the quality of the generated highlights and narratives, and used the graph interface to search, navigate, and interpret the match highlights. These results provide early evidence that one small, human-readable schema can ground agent generation and support human interpretation at the same time.

</details>


### [27] [The Data Hospital: A Workflow-Based Concept for Explainable Research Data Quality Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20782)
*Lennard Scheurer,Robert Porzel,Vinicius Carrillo Beber,Rainer Malaka*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"数据医院"（Data Hospital）概念，一个以人为中心、循环控制的研究数据质量交互模型，采用医院隐喻构建十阶段工作流，使可评估性、不确定性、干预权限、溯源与流程可复现性显性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究数据质量是多维度且依赖目的的，它产生于数据、预期用途、语境知识、文档、干预决策与溯源之间的相互作用，现有方法难以系统性地呈现评估、不确定性与干预权限等要素。

Method: 采用医院隐喻设计十阶段工作流：数据被接纳、语境化、评估、在专门"科室"中审查，仅通过批准干预进行修改，随后验证、记录，并在干预描述充分时实现可回放。方法将确定性画像与可审查证据，与可选的、绑定证据的 Dr. Data 解释及用户显式决策相结合；通过保留原始数据与受控工作状态分离观察与干预。

Result: 原型为有实现支撑的演示器，而非正式发布的研究工件，通过表征标准化、插补、"患者档案"文档与回放展示了该概念的选定部分。

Conclusion: 该研究的贡献不在于新的清洗或插补算法，而在于一个使评估性、不确定性、干预权限、溯源与流程可复现性可见的十阶段工作流，并提出后续技术与以用户为中心的评估分阶段议程。

Abstract: Research data quality is multidimensional and purpose-dependent: it emerges from the interplay of data, intended use, contextual knowledge, documentation, intervention decisions, and traceability. This concept paper presents the Data Hospital, a human-in-the-loop control and interaction model for research data quality. Using a hospital metaphor, datasets are admitted, contextualized, assessed, reviewed in specialized stations, modified only through approved interventions, validated, documented, and made replayable where interventions are sufficiently specified. The concept combines deterministic profiling and inspectable evidence with optional evidence-bound explanation by Dr. Data and explicit user decisions. Preserved Raw Data and controlled working states separate observation from intervention. The contribution is not a new cleaning or imputation algorithm, but a ten-stage workflow that makes assessability, uncertainty, intervention authority, provenance, and process reproducibility visible. The prototype is an implementation-backed demonstrator rather than a released research artifact and illustrates selected parts of the concept through representational standardization, imputation, Patient File documentation, and replay. The paper concludes with a staged agenda for subsequent technical and user-centered evaluation.

</details>

### [28] [Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning: Stability under Constant Stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19170)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Skylar Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文构造了ETD采样动力学不稳定的二状态反例，并提出正则化强调式TD（RETD）以实现稳定收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ETD虽稳定了期望离策略TD更新并改变投影几何，但这两点都不能决定常数步长下的采样动力学；作者要研究采样层面是否真正稳定。

Method: 构造遍历二状态反例；用再生周期分析将符号与follow-on迹的无限方差分离；提出RETD——归一化一阶post-shock修复，保持迹和重要性比不变，用泄漏标量状态存储ETD信号并释放延迟校正；证明谐波递减步长下的几乎必然收敛及基于马尔可夫随机乘积界的条件常数步长矩收缩；进行配对10,000次实验。

Result: ETD均值映射收缩但采样乘积顶部Lyapunov指数为正；RETD原始均衡为ETD均衡的仿射平移，单/双正则读出精确恢复ETD不动点；RETD在二状态构造和一个Baird点上有认证负指数，Baird ETD正号仍为数值；实验验证两种分离、不动点恢复、非单调稳定区和任务依赖。

Conclusion: RETD改变post-shock动力学，但不降低共享follow-on-trace方差；说明稳定采样动力学需新的校正机制而非仅依赖ETD几何性质。

Abstract: Emphatic temporal-difference learning (ETD) stabilizes the expected off-policy TD update and changes its projection geometry, but neither property determines constant-stepsize sampled dynamics. We construct an ergodic two-state counterexample in which the ETD mean map contracts while the sampled product has a positive top Lyapunov exponent. Regenerative-cycle analysis separates this sign from the infinite variance of the follow-on trace. We introduce regularized emphatic TD (RETD), a normalized first-order post-shock repair that leaves the trace and importance ratios unchanged, stores the emphatic TD signal in a leaky scalar state, and releases a delayed correction. RETD's raw equilibrium is an affine shift of the ETD equilibrium; single- and two-regularization readouts recover the ETD fixed point exactly. We prove almost-sure convergence for harmonic diminishing stepsizes and a conditional constant-stepsize moment-contraction result from a Markovian random-product bound. RETD has certified negative exponents on the two-state construction and one Baird point, whereas the positive Baird ETD sign remains numerical. Paired 10,000-run experiments validate both separations, fixed-point recovery, a nonmonotone stability region, and task dependence. RETD changes post-shock dynamics; it does not reduce the shared follow-on-trace variance.

</details>


### [29] [BioPhys-Bridge: A Benchmark for Interdisciplinary Scientific Reasoning in Physics-Grounded Biological Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19180)
*Qingyang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BioPhys-Bridge，一个面向生物物理文献、强调证据溯源的科学推理基准数据集，包含 500 个案例、1,517 个任务，覆盖六大生物领域与九类物理模型族，并给出初步评测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在跨学科科研文献分析中面临独特挑战：生物物理研究的可靠回答需将观测数据锚定到原文证据，并通过定量物理模型解读，再关联到生物机制。现有基准难以同时考察证据溯源、定量推断与机制映射，因此需要一个专门的基准来评测此类多步科学推理。

Method: 构建 BioPhys-Bridge 基准数据集。每个案例包含证据块、稳定的证据 ID、定量数值与单位、方程、假设、机制以及下一步决策，作为问答（QA）与检索增强生成（RAG）的锚定目标。初始版本含 500 个案例、1,517 个智能体任务，覆盖六大生物领域和九类物理模型族（其中三个稀疏族保留待扩展）。数据构建中实施严格质量门控，包括模式校验、证据完整性、定量锚定、来源许可、去重、单位归一化，并对 81 个案例进行领域专家评审与标注。

Result: 初步评测显示，DeepSeek-V4-Flash 获得最高的证据 ID 的 F1 分数（0.360），其后为 Qwen3.7-Max（0.316）与 GPT-4o-mini（0.294）。

Conclusion: BioPhys-Bridge 是一个跨学科基准，用于评估复杂多步科学推理中的归因能力、忠实度、幻觉抑制以及生物实验设计。未来工作将扩大数据集规模与复杂度，并开展更全面的评测。代码与数据已在 GitHub 仓库和 Hugging Face 上公开。

Abstract: Language models face unique challenges in analyzing interdisciplinary scientific research literature. In biophysics research, faithful answers require grounding observed data in source evidence, interpreting it through a quantitative physics model, and linking it to a biological mechanism. To address this challenge, we introduce BioPhys-Bridge, a novel benchmark dataset for evidence-grounded scientific reasoning over biophysical literature. Each case contains evidence blocks, stable evidence IDs, quantitative values, units, equations, assumptions, mechanisms, and next decisions as grounding targets for question answering (QA) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The initial release contains 500 cases, 1,517 agent-facing tasks, and covers six biological domains and nine physical model families, including three sparse families reserved for future expansion. We enforce strict quality gates for all cases in schema, evidence-integrity, quantitative-grounding, source-license, duplicate, unit-normalization, with domain expert review and annotation for 81 cases. Preliminary evaluations show that DeepSeek-V4-Flash obtain the highest evidence-ID $F_1$ score (0.360), followed by Qwen3.7-Max (0.316) and GPT-4o-mini (0.294). BioPhys-Bridge is an interdisciplinary benchmark for evaluating attribution, faithfulness, hallucination reduction, and biological experiment design with complex, multi-step scientific reasoning. Future works will increase the size and complexity of the dataset and perform comprehensive evaluations. Code and data are available in the GitHub repository and on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [30] [What Do We Expect from LLMs? Mapping the Design of LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19182)
*Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统梳理了2022年1月至2026年8月arXiv上14,767篇评估资源论文，揭示LLM评估需求的变化趋势：目标系统与领域、评估材料与条件、评分机制，发现对行动、交互与专业应用的关注增长，且模型参与评估的程度发展不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准排名只反映模型表现，无法说明评估需求本身如何演变。作者希望从基准的多样变化中，理解研究者对LLM能力期望与成功标准的转变。

Method: 对2022年1月至2026年8月arXiv上14,767篇引入或更新评估资源的论文进行系统映射，采用分阶段筛选与自动化全文编码，分析目标系统与领域、评估材料与条件、评分机制的变化。

Result: 论文集合显示对行动、交互和专业应用的关注不断增强，既有与新兴设计元素常并存；模型参与发展不均：基于LLM的评分在智能体与非智能体组中均增长，而模型生成材料在近期队列中无持续增长。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了公共研究如何将能力期望转化为具体测试与成功标准，并提出关键问题：随着AI参与构建测试、执行任务和评判回答，扩展的评估究竟提供了更多独立证据，还是可能复制参与模型的偏好与盲点。

Abstract: Benchmarks are central to how progress in large language models (LLMs) is assessed and communicated. Yet model rankings alone reveal little about how evaluation requirements themselves are changing. The expanding variety of benchmarks offers another perspective: what researchers expect LLMs to do, and what they count as successful performance. We systematically map 14,767 papers introducing or updating evaluation resources from arXiv submissions between January 2022 and August 2026. Using staged screening and automated full-text coding, we examine changes in target systems and domains, evaluation materials and conditions, and scoring mechanisms. The collection shows growing emphasis on action, interaction, and professional applications, while established and newer design elements frequently coexist. Model participation also develops unevenly: LLM-based scoring grows within both agent and non-agent groups, whereas model-generated materials show no comparable sustained increase in recent cohorts. These findings illuminate how public research translates capability expectations into concrete tests and criteria for success. As AI participates in constructing tests, performing tasks, and judging responses, they also raise a question: does expanding evaluation provide more independent evidence, or risk reproducing the preferences and blind spots of its participating models?

</details>


### [31] [Position: It is Time to Virtualize Foundation Models with a Self-evolving Operating System Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19203)
*Suparna Bhattacharya,Tarun Kumar,Cong Xu,Satish Kumar Mopur,Jiahao Li,Ashish Mishra,Aalap Tripathy,Annmary Justine Koomthanam,Martin Foltin,Ian Foster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张AI领域需要一个"基础模型操作系统"（FMOS），作为虚拟化基础模型交互的系统层，解决当前智能体栈碎片化、行为不可移植与治理脆弱的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI应用已从单一基础模型转向复合智能体系统，但栈仍高度碎片化：尽管MCP、A2A等协议简化了工具与智能体连接，各框架仍内嵌各自的状态、记忆、预算与护栏运行时，导致行为无法移植、治理脆弱。这种局面类似于操作系统出现前的计算机时代，每个程序都要重复实现基础服务。

Method: 作为立场论文，文章提出FMOS概念：类比虚拟机抽象物理硬件，为应用提供"独占、可信、能力近乎无限"的基础模型实例幻象。FMOS内部负责跨内存层级的知识编排、模型选择与资源分配、以及验证与策略执行；并借鉴人脑快直觉与慢思考的切换机制，学习何时介入、何时让推理直接进行，并基于运行经验持续调整策略。

Result: 本文为立场性论述，未提供实验或实证结果，主要通过类比论证与体系结构构想阐述FMOS的必要性与构成。

Conclusion: 该领域需要构建FMOS这一系统层，以统一虚拟化基础模型交互，实现可移植行为、稳健治理与自适应编排。

Abstract: AI applications have shifted from single, monolithic foundation models (FM) to compound agentic systems. Yet today's stacks remain fragmented: even as protocols (e.g., MCP, A2A) ease tool/agent connectivity, each framework embeds an implicit runtime for state, memory, budgets, and guardrails, making behavior non-portable and governance brittle. It mirrors computing before operating systems, when every program re-implemented basic services. This position paper argues that the field now needs a Foundation Model Operating System (FMOS) -- a system layer that virtualizes FM interactions analogous to how virtual machines abstract physical hardware, giving applications the illusion of dedicated, trustworthy FM instances with effectively unbounded capabilities. Internally, the FMOS orchestrates knowledge across memory tiers, model selection and resource allocation, and verification and policy enforcement. Like the human brain switching between fast intuition and slow deliberation, the FMOS learns when to intervene and when to let inference proceed directly and continuously adapting its policies based on operational experience.

</details>


### [32] [What Do Current Systematic Generalization Tasks Miss? A Reasoning-Centered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19212)
*Chengwen Qi,Deheng Ye,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 TranSGrid 测试平台，将演绎、归纳与溯因推理统一到同一任务中，用于更全面地衡量系统泛化能力，并揭示现有任务的简化局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统泛化（通过重组已知原子元素解决新问题）是人类智能的核心，但在受控条件下难以严格研究。现有研究依赖近似线性动作组合、基于生产力的测试、动作显式目标等简化，虽便于研究却遗漏了该能力的关键方面。作者希望刻画这些简化究竟遗漏了什么。

Method: 以推理为中心视角，提出 TranSGrid 测试平台，在同一任务中融合演绎、归纳和溯因三种推理。在 4,800 个 TranSGrid 实例上测试七个 Transformer 模型，并设计两个变体：一个使动作组合近似线性（降低归纳需求），另一个使目标动作显式化（降低溯因需求）。

Result: 所有模型在 TranSGrid 上的表现远差于留出测试集：最大模型在测试集上解决 79.6%，但在 TranSGrid 上仅 55.3%，最难子集仅 15.8%。差距即使在训练长度范围内仍存在，说明单靠生产力不足以评估系统泛化。引入两个简化变体后，解决率均回升至接近测试集水平，表明任一简化都足以将 TranSGrid 降为普通留出测试集。

Conclusion: 现有任务降低或同时降低了归纳与溯因需求。要全面衡量系统泛化，任务需要同时涉及演绎、归纳与溯因三种推理形式。

Abstract: Systematic generalization, the ability to solve novel problems by recombining known atomic elements, is central to human intelligence but difficult to study rigorously under controlled settings. Existing studies therefore rely on simplifications such as approximately linear action composition, productivity-based tests, and action-explicit goals, which make systematic generalization easier to study but omit some essential aspects of this capability. To characterize what these simplifications miss, we adopt a reasoning-centered lens and introduce TranSGrid, a testbed that brings deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning together within a unified task. Experiments with seven Transformers on 4,800 TranSGrid instances show that all models perform much worse on TranSGrid than on a held-out test set: the largest model solves 79.6% of the test set, but only 55.3% of TranSGrid and 15.8% of the hardest subset. The gap remains within the training length range, showing that productivity alone is not sufficient to evaluate systematic generalization. Additionally, we reintroduce the other two simplifications into TranSGrid: one variant makes actions compose almost linearly (reducing the inductive demand), the other makes goals action-explicit (reducing the abductive one). In both, solve rates return to roughly the test set level, showing that either simplification alone is enough to reduce TranSGrid to an ordinary held-out test set. Together, our results show that existing tasks reduce either or both of the inductive and abductive demands, and that comprehensively measuring systematic generalization requires a task that involves all three forms of reasoning.

</details>


### [33] [Characterizing Web Search by Conversational LLM Agents: From Search Decisions and Strategies to Results and Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19244)
*Mahsa Amani,Seungeon Lee,Abhisek Dash,Asmaa El Fraihi,Yunah Jang,Elisabeth Kirsten,Qinyuan Wu,Krishna P. Gummadi,Manish Gupta,Abhilasha Ravichander,Muhammad Bilal Zafar,Soumi Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个跨ChatGPT、Claude、Grok、DeepSeek四大平台的智能体式Web搜索生命周期研究，结合真实用户交互与API受控实验，考察搜索调用决策、查询策略、域名偏好及结果落地，发现平台差异显著、搜索越多未必越好、存在未引用主张等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式LLM智能体日益依赖Web搜索，但其智能体式搜索的端到端生命周期仍缺乏系统理解，亟需厘清搜索调用决策、查询构建、结果来源及回答生成等环节的机制与差异。

Method: 研究覆盖ChatGPT、Claude、Grok、DeepSeek四大对话平台；采用真实用户交互（in vivo）与通过各平台API对同一模型进行受控实验（in vitro）相结合的方法；分析四个环节：是否调用Web搜索的决策质量、查询构建策略、搜索结果中的潜在域名偏好、将搜索结果转化为有依据回答时的选择。

Result: Web搜索决策在各平台与模型间差异显著，更频繁调用搜索并不必然带来更高质量回答；对话智能体采用不同的复杂查询策略；各平台专属搜索引擎倾向于返回其偏好域名的结果；回答虽大体基于搜索结果，但部分主张依赖未被引用的检索结果，引发归属与可靠性担忧。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了对话式智能体Web搜索全生命周期的平台差异与可靠性隐患，对未来面向对话式检索优化的AI智能体与Web搜索工具设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Conversational LLM agents increasingly rely on Web search, yet the end-to-end lifecycle of agentic search remains poorly understood. We present the first study of Web search across four major conversational platforms (ChatGPT, Claude, Grok, and DeepSeek), combining real-world user interactions (invivo) with controlled experiments using the same platform's models by their APIs (invitro). We investigate the quality of agentic decisions to invoke Web search, their strategies to formulate queries, the potential domain preferences in the search results they receive, and the choices they make when transforming search results into grounded responses. We find that Web-search decisions vary substantially across platforms and models, while more frequent Web-search invocation does not necessarily yield better response quality. We further show that conversational agents employ different complex querying strategies and that platform specific search engines return search results from their preferred domains. Finally, although responses are largely grounded in search results, some claims rely on uncited search results, raising concerns about attribution and reliability. Our findings have important implications for the design of future AI agents and Web search tools optimized for conversational retrieval.

</details>


### [34] [Do AI Agents Understand Computer Architecture?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19387)
*Ambika Sharan,Grigory Chirkov,Soheil Abbasloo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AutoTuring 评测框架：让同一智能体在相同 15 维加速器设计空间上分别面对"有语义的架构旋钮"与"匿名 [0,1] 变量"两种表述，从而把"智能体是否真正理解机器"与"仅仅在无意义旋钮上做搜索"区分开来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于智能体设计硬件的报道只能证明设计变好了，却无法说明为什么变好：智能体可能真的在对机器结构进行推理，也可能只是熟练地在它从未理解其含义的旋钮上搜索，而只有前者才能迁移到下一个架构。既有评测方法都通过改变智能体、固定问题表述来比较，因而无法区分这两种情形。

Method: 作者反过来固定智能体、改变问题表述。AutoTuring 将同一个智能体、同一个 15 维加速器空间交给它两次：一次以带名称的架构旋钮加模拟器计数器形式呈现，一次以 [0,1] 上的匿名变量呈现；两次评测器、合法空间与可达最优解完全一致，唯一变化的是问题是否具有语义。两者之间的差距即为所要测量的量。

Result: 在九核 FP16 GEMM 测试集上，语义是有回报的：架构智能体平均优于建模的 H200 约 5.4%，优于其"盲版"对手约 12.3%，且模拟器调用次数少 70.1%。但语义的回报并非独有：一个 critic 循环为盲智能体找回了大部分差距，而对架构智能体毫无增益，说明架构知识与结构化批评之间存在替代关系而非互补关系。

Conclusion: 作者将结论定位为初步发现（每条件 5 至 6 次运行、单一建模加速器），并强调真正的贡献是这一比较方法本身，而非所得到的加速器设计。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly asked to design hardware, and increasingly reported to succeed. Such reports establish that a design improved; they cannot establish why. An agent that improves an accelerator may be reasoning about the machine, or may be searching competently over knobs whose meaning it never recovers -- and only the first transfers to the next architecture. Existing evaluations cannot tell the two apart, because they vary the agent while holding the framing of the problem fixed. We do the opposite. AutoTuring hands the same agent the same 15-dimensional accelerator space twice: once as named architectural knobs with simulator counters, once as anonymous variables on [0,1], with the evaluator, the legal space and the reachable optima held identical, so that the only thing that varies is whether the problem means anything. The gap between the two is the measurement. On a nine-kernel FP16 GEMM basket, meaning pays: the architect beats a modeled H200 by 5.4% and its blind counterpart by 12.3% on average, with 70.1% fewer simulator calls. It does not pay uniquely: a critic loop recovers most of that gap for the blind agent and buys the architect nothing, so architectural knowledge and structured critique behave as substitutes rather than as complements. We report these as preliminary findings -- five to six runs per condition on a single modeled accelerator -- and take the comparison itself, not the accelerator, to be the contribution.

</details>


### [35] [MAGS: Multi-agent Auto-formalization Guarantees Safety for Agentic Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19391)
*Albert Wu,Nicholas Roberts,Tzu-Heng Huang,Haoran Lin,Gil Friedman,Sungjun Cho,Gabriel Orlanski,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGS是一个多智能体框架，借助Dafny作为验证中间表示，将LLM生成的代码自动形式化验证并编译回可执行程序，在220个任务上实现了100%的安全保证生成成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理生成的复杂程序规模庞大，人工审查难以全面覆盖，模糊测试、静态分析和LLM作为验证器等方法难以覆盖所有边界情况，而传统形式化验证又需要大量人工规约与证明工程，因此需要一种能自动生成带形式化安全保障程序的方法。

Method: 提出统一的MAGS多智能体框架：冻结经人工审计的API与安全需求，将LLM生成的代码翻译为Dafny验证感知中间表示，利用验证器反馈修复违规，再将验证通过的程序编译回可执行代码。

Result: 在100个CUDA内核、100个终端脚本和20个机器人臂任务共220个示例上，针对冻结规约实现了100%的非平凡安全保证生成成功率；独立的安全性与功能性评估在三个领域均表现强劲，同时揭示了当自动形式化语义未能完全捕捉目标行为时出现的失败。

Conclusion: MAGS表明多智能体结合验证感知中间表示可有效为LLM生成代码提供机器可检验的安全保障，但自动形式化语义的准确性仍是局限，需进一步保证语义捕获的完备性。

Abstract: LLM coding agents now generate complex programs at a scale that makes thorough human review increasingly difficult, raising the risk of safety and security failures. Common approaches, including fuzz testing, static analysis, and LLM-as-a-Verifier, can detect many failures but struggle to cover all possible edge cases. Formal verification addresses this by providing machine-checkable guarantees over specified properties, but traditionally demands substantial manual specification and proof engineering. We introduce a unified multi-agent framework, MAGS, that generates executable programs with formal safety guarantees, using Dafny as a verification-aware intermediate representation where safety properties can be mechanically checked. MAGS formalizes and freezes human-audited APIs and safety requirements, translates generated code into Dafny, repairs violations using verifier feedback, and compiles verified programs back into executable code. We evaluate MAGS on 100 CUDA kernels, 100 terminal scripts, and 20 robotic-arm tasks. Across all 220 examples, it achieves a 100% success rate in producing programs with non-trivial safety guarantees against frozen specifications. Independent safety and functional evaluations further show strong performance across all three domains, while revealing failures when the auto-formalized semantics do not fully capture the target behavior.

</details>


### [36] [Closed-World Resolution Against Tool Hallucination in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19425)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是测量与基准研究，揭示工具增强LLM智能体的"工具幻觉"盲区：智能体会调用不存在的工具、传参不符合任何schema，而现有工具选择与安全门控方法均无法拦截。作者提出五类分类法（H1-H5）、无训练闭世界解析器（Resolution Rung），并证明幻觉防御必须先于因果门控。在10个模型两种调用面上测得322次真实幻觉，扩展到MCP后又测得154次（M1-M5），并发布HTB基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御分为工具选择与工具门控两类，但二者都预设"所发出的调用指向真实工具"。对于幻觉调用，它按构造就不是任何门控所作出的决策，因此没有门控能拒绝它——这是结构性盲区。作者希望测量并刻画这一问题，并提供可比较的基准。

Method: 1) 提出H1-H5五类工具幻觉分类法；2) 给出Resolution Rung：免训练、闭世界的解析器（注册表成员检查+签名校验），其价值在于"必须位于何处"而非计算内容；3) 证明幻觉防御必须先于因果门控，并刻画唯一不可约残留（借用的参数在schema上与合法调用无法区分）；4) 在10个托管模型、两种调用面上测量；5) 扩展到Model Context Protocol，提出M1-M5第二套分类法；6) 发布带版本的HTB基准。

Result: 测得322次真实幻觉：伪造工具调用集中在无约束的raw-JSON面（34次 vs 3次）；模型规模无帮助（675B模型与7-8B相当）。扩展到MCP后测得154次幻觉，且多服务器合并命名空间导致的碰撞与遮蔽是结构性的，单注册表无法表达，连在单注册表面表现干净的前沿模型也出现幻觉。

Conclusion: 工具幻觉是一个结构性盲区，必须在任何因果门控之前进行防御；仅靠工具选择或门控不充分，且模型规模无法解决。多服务器命名空间合并会引入单注册表无法表达的幻觉面。作者发布HTB基准以支持不同解析器跨提交比较。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model (LLM) agents fail in a way no tool-selection or tool-security method addresses: they call tools that do not exist and pass arguments no schema declares. Existing defenses either pick the right tool (selection) or constrain what an agent may do with real tools (gating), both of which presuppose the emitted call refers to a real tool at all. We show this is a structural blind spot: a hallucinated call is by construction not a decision any gate made, so no gate can reject it. This paper is primarily a measurement and benchmark study. We give a five-class taxonomy of tool hallucination (H1-H5) and, as a reference point, the Resolution Rung: a training-free, closed-world resolver (registry membership plus a signature check) whose interest is where it must sit, not what it computes. We prove hallucination defense must precede any causal gate, and characterize the one irreducible residue (borrowed arguments schema-indistinguishable from a valid call). Across ten hosted models under two invocation surfaces we measure 322 genuine hallucinations; fabricated-tool calls concentrate on the unconstrained raw-JSON surface (34 vs. 3), and model scale does not help (a 675B model matches a 7-8B one). We then extend to the Model Context Protocol, where merging several servers into one namespace creates hallucination surfaces a single registry cannot express (a second taxonomy, M1-M5); on the live MCP surface we measure 154 hallucinations, including from frontier models that were clean on the single-registry surface, because collisions and shadowing are structural to the merge. We release the versioned Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark (HTB) so any resolver is comparable across submissions.

</details>


### [37] [The syntax and semantics of goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19448)
*David M. Abel,Mark K. Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将"目标"视为可组合的认知表征，类比语言学的句法-语义界面，系统梳理不同目标表征的性质，并提出一个关于目标表征的设计空间框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在认知科学与计算机科学中，目标通常被当作固定且施加行为约束的认知状态，但对其作为"表征"本身的性质（如表达力、设计、效率）缺乏系统关注。作者希望通过强调目标的内容与形式，揭示与语言学、逻辑学中句法-语义界面的平行关系，并凸显关于目标表征表达力与效率的基础性问题。

Method: 采用概念综合与理论梳理的方法：将目标界定为可组合的表征，其内容关联理性行为；对比语言学与逻辑学的句法-语义界面，分析不同目标表征在表达力、设计、效率方面的属性及约束；综合相关研究，将不同目标表征界定为更广泛设计空间的若干要点。

Result: 识别出目标表征的多重属性维度，例如某一目标语言是否有足够表达力以捕捉所关注的行为，以及不同目标表征是否刻画相同行为，从而把它们组织成一个统一的设计空间。

Conclusion: 区分目标的"形式"与"意义"有助于澄清我们对目标所作的隐含假设，促进对高层认知与动机之间交互的研究，并分离出不同目标概念之间的变异轴。

Abstract: In both cognitive science and computer science, goals are conceptualized as cognitive states that flexibly combine with world knowledge to organize and specify purposeful behavior. In this way, goals are compositional representations whose content relates to rational behavior. We here draw attention to goals as representations and their content because it highlights a parallel with other areas in cognitive science - in particular, the syntax-semantics interface in linguistics and logic - while also foregrounding foundational questions about the expressivity, design, and efficiency of different goal representations. For example, goals are typically taken as fixed and imposing constraints on desirable behaviors, but we can also identify constraints on goal representations themselves, such as whether a particular goal language is sufficiently expressive to capture behaviors of interest, or whether different goal representations capture the same behavior. Here, we synthesize work that aims to characterize the properties of different goal representations and suggest these are points of a broader design space. We close by discussing how distinguishing the form and meaning of goals can elucidate the implicit assumptions we make about goals, inform the study of interactions between higher-level cognition and motivation, and isolate axes of variation for different conceptions of goals.

</details>
